                            );
                            copy_16x8(planes.ref_cr, c_off, c_s, planes.dst_cr, c_off, c_s);
                            copy_16x8(planes.ref_cb, c_off, c_s, planes.dst_cb, c_off, c_s);
                        } else {
                            copy_16x16(planes.ref_y, y_off + 16, ya_s, &mut scratch_y, 0, 16);
                            copy_16x8(planes.ref_cr, c_off, c_s, &mut scratch_cr, 0, 16);
                            copy_16x8(planes.ref_cb, c_off, c_s, &mut scratch_cb, 0, 16);
                        }
                        if (f & 3) == 0 {
                            let y16 = ya_s * 16;
                            let c8 = c_s * 8;
                            copy_16x16(
                                planes.ref_y,
                                y_off + y16,
                                ya_s,
                                planes.dst_y,
                                y_off + y16,
                                ya_s,
                            );
                            copy_16x16(
                                planes.ref_y,
                                y_off + y16 + 16,
                                ya_s,
                                planes.dst_y,
                                y_off + y16 + 16,
                                ya_s,
                            );
                            copy_16x8(
                                planes.ref_cr,
                                c_off + c8,
                                c_s,
                                planes.dst_cr,
                                c_off + c8,
                                c_s,
                            );
                            copy_16x8(
                                planes.ref_cb,
                                c_off + c8,
                                c_s,
                                planes.dst_cb,
                                c_off + c8,
                                c_s,
                            );
                        } else if (f & 1) == 0 {
                            copy_16x16(
                                planes.ref_y,
                                y_off + ya_s * 16 + 16,
                                ya_s,
                                &mut scratch_y,
                                0,
                                16,
                            );
                            copy_16x8(planes.ref_cr, c_off + c_s * 8, c_s, &mut scratch_cr, 0, 16);
                            copy_16x8(planes.ref_cb, c_off + c_s * 8, c_s, &mut scratch_cb, 0, 16);
                        } else {
                            copy_16x16(planes.ref_y, y_off + 16, ya_s, &mut scratch_y, 0, 16);
                            copy_16x8(planes.ref_cr, c_off, c_s, &mut scratch_cr, 0, 16);
                            copy_16x8(planes.ref_cb, c_off, c_s, &mut scratch_cb, 0, 16);
                        }
                        if alpha_coded {
                            if let (Some(ref mut da), Some(ra)) = (&mut planes.dst_a, planes.ref_a) {
                                copy_skip_plane16(ra, da, y_off, ya_s, f, &mut scratch_a);
                            }
                        }
                        if hdr_coded {
                            if let (Some(ref mut dh), Some(rh)) = (&mut planes.dst_h, planes.ref_h) {
                                copy_skip_plane16(rh, dh, y_off, ya_s, f, &mut scratch_h);
                            }
                        }
                        mv_cur[mx * 8..mx * 8 + 8].fill(0);
                    }
                    2 => {
                        // Inter MC-only (zero residual)
                        decode_mvs(&mut bs, mv_cur, mv_above, mx, mb_flags);
                        let cmv = clamp_mvs(mv_cur, mx, x_px as i32, y_px as i32, w2, h2, mb_flags);
                        mc_only_plane16(planes.ref_y, planes.dst_y, y_off, ya_s, &cmv, f, &mut scratch_y);
                        mc_only_chroma(
                            planes.ref_cr,
                            planes.dst_cr,
                            planes.ref_cb,
                            planes.dst_cb,
                            c_off,
                            c_s,
                            &cmv,
                            f,
                            &mut scratch_cr,
                            &mut scratch_cb,
                        );
                        if alpha_coded {
                            if let (Some(ref mut da), Some(ra)) = (&mut planes.dst_a, planes.ref_a) {
                                mc_only_plane16(ra, da, y_off, ya_s, &cmv, f, &mut scratch_a);
                            }
                        }
                        if hdr_coded {
                            if let (Some(ref mut dh), Some(rh)) = (&mut planes.dst_h, planes.ref_h) {
                                mc_only_plane16(rh, dh, y_off, ya_s, &cmv, f, &mut scratch_h);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    3 => {
                        // Inter MC + residual
                        decode_mvs(&mut bs, mv_cur, mv_above, mx, mb_flags);
                        let cmv = clamp_mvs(mv_cur, mx, x_px as i32, y_px as i32, w2, h2, mb_flags);
                        let q_val = (decode_qdelta(&mut bs, q_cur[mx][1] as i32) & 0xff) as u32;
                        q_cur[mx][1] = q_val as u8;

                        decode_inter_plane16(
                            Some((planes.dst_y, y_off, ya_s)),
                            Some((planes.ref_y, ya_s)),
                            &mut bs,
                            plane_flags,
                            &cmv,
                            q_val,
                            &mut cbp_inter_y,
                            &mut scratch_y,
                        );

                        if bs.read_bit() == 0 {
                            cbp_inter_cr = 0;
                            cbp_inter_cb = 0;
                            mc_only_chroma(
                                planes.ref_cr,
                                planes.dst_cr,
                                planes.ref_cb,
                                planes.dst_cb,
                                c_off,
                                c_s,
                                &cmv,
                                f,
                                &mut scratch_cr,
                                &mut scratch_cb,
                            );
                        } else {
                            decode_inter_plane4(
                                planes.dst_cr,
                                c_off,
                                c_s,
                                planes.ref_cr,
                                c_s,
                                &mut bs,
                                &cmv,
                                plane_flags,
                                q_val,
                                &mut cbp_inter_cr,
                                &mut scratch_cr,
                            );
                            decode_inter_plane4(
                                planes.dst_cb,
                                c_off,
                                c_s,
                                planes.ref_cb,
                                c_s,
                                &mut bs,
                                &cmv,
                                plane_flags,
                                q_val,
                                &mut cbp_inter_cb,
                                &mut scratch_cb,
                            );
                        }

                        if alpha_coded {
                            let da_opt = planes.dst_a.as_deref_mut().map(|b| (b, y_off, ya_s));
                            let ra_opt = planes.ref_a.map(|b| (b, ya_s));
                            decode_inter_plane16(
                                da_opt,
                                ra_opt,
                                &mut bs,
                                plane_flags,
                                &cmv,
                                q_val,
                                &mut cbp_inter_a,
                                &mut scratch_a,
                            );
                        }
                        if hdr_coded {
                            let dh_opt = planes.dst_h.as_deref_mut().map(|b| (b, y_off, ya_s));
                            let rh_opt = planes.ref_h.map(|b| (b, ya_s));
                            decode_inter_plane16(
                                dh_opt,
                                rh_opt,
                                &mut bs,
                                plane_flags,
                                &cmv,
                                q_val,
                                &mut cbp_inter_h,
                                &mut scratch_h,
                            );
                        }
                    }
                    _ => unreachable!(),
                }

                postfilter_mb(
                    &mut pf,
                    &mut y_ab2,
                    &mut y_ab1,
                    &mut a_ab2,
                    &mut a_ab1,
                    &mut h_ab2,
                    &mut h_ab1,
                    &mut cr_ab2,
                    &mut cr_ab1,
                    &mut cb_ab2,
                    &mut cb_ab1,
                    &cur_y_info,
                    &cur_cr_info,
                    &cur_cb_info,
                    &cur_a_info,
                    &cur_h_info,
                    mb_flags,
                    planes,
                    mx,
                    y_px,
                    mv_cur,
                    mv_above,
                    thresh,
                );

                // Predict Q for next MB on this row
                if mx + 1 < mb_cols {
                    if (mb_flags & 0x88) == 0 {
                        q_cur[mx + 1][0] =
                            median3_u8(q_above[mx][0], q_above[mx + 1][0], q_cur[mx][0]);
                        q_cur[mx + 1][1] =
                            median3_u8(q_above[mx][1], q_above[mx + 1][1], q_cur[mx][1]);
                    } else {
                        q_cur[mx + 1] = q_cur[mx];
                    }
                }
            }

            cur_is_a = !cur_is_a;
            row_carry = ((row_flags & 4) << 2) | (row_flags & 0xffff_fe60);
            row_flags_state = row_carry;
            y_px = next_y;
        }

        cur_slice += 1;
        row_carry = 0;
        bs.align_u32();
        if cur_slice < slices.num_slices as usize {
            let expected_off = 4 + (cur_slice - 1) * 4;
            if expected_off + 4 <= hdr_offset {
                let hdr_slice_end = u32::from_le_bytes([
                    packet[expected_off],
                    packet[expected_off + 1],
                    packet[expected_off + 2],
                    packet[expected_off + 3],
                ]) as usize;
                let actual = bs.byte_pos();
                if actual != hdr_slice_end {
                    log::warn!(
                        "bink2: slice {cur_slice} pos={actual:#x}, header_val={hdr_slice_end:#x} (pkt_len={:#x})",
                        packet.len()
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

#[inline]
fn copy_skip_plane16(
    ref_buf: &[u8],
    dst_buf: &mut [u8],
    off: usize,
    stride: usize,
    f: u32,
    scratch: &mut [u8; 256],
) {
    if (f & 4) == 0 {
        copy_16x16(ref_buf, off, stride, dst_buf, off, stride);
        copy_16x16(ref_buf, off + 16, stride, dst_buf, off + 16, stride);
    } else {
        copy_16x16(ref_buf, off + 16, stride, scratch, 0, 16);
    }
    if (f & 3) == 0 {
        let s16 = stride * 16;
        copy_16x16(ref_buf, off + s16, stride, dst_buf, off + s16, stride);
        copy_16x16(ref_buf, off + s16 + 16, stride, dst_buf, off + s16 + 16, stride);
    } else if (f & 1) == 0 {
        copy_16x16(ref_buf, off + stride * 16 + 16, stride, scratch, 0, 16);
    } else {
        copy_16x16(ref_buf, off + 16, stride, scratch, 0, 16);
    }
}

#[inline]
fn mc_only_plane16(
    ref_buf: &[u8],
    dst_buf: &mut [u8],
    off: usize,
    stride: usize,
    cmv: &[u32; 8],
    f: u32,
    scratch: &mut [u8; 256],
) {
    if (f & 4) == 0 {
        mc_luma_16x16(
            ref_buf,
            stride,
            (cmv[0] >> 1) as i32,
            (cmv[1] >> 1) as i32,
            dst_buf,
            off,
            stride,
            (cmv[0] & 1) != 0,
            (cmv[1] & 1) != 0,
        );
        mc_luma_16x16(
            ref_buf,
            stride,
            (cmv[2] >> 1) as i32,
            (cmv[3] >> 1) as i32,
            dst_buf,
            off + 16,
            stride,
            (cmv[2] & 1) != 0,
            (cmv[3] & 1) != 0,
        );
    } else {
        mc_luma_16x16(
            ref_buf,
            stride,
            (cmv[2] >> 1) as i32,
            (cmv[3] >> 1) as i32,
            scratch,
            0,
            16,
            (cmv[2] & 1) != 0,
            (cmv[3] & 1) != 0,
        );
    }
    if (f & 3) == 0 {
        let s16 = stride * 16;
        mc_luma_16x16(
            ref_buf,
            stride,
            (cmv[4] >> 1) as i32,
            (cmv[5] >> 1) as i32,
            dst_buf,
            off + s16,
            stride,
            (cmv[4] & 1) != 0,
            (cmv[5] & 1) != 0,
        );
        mc_luma_16x16(
            ref_buf,
            stride,
            (cmv[6] >> 1) as i32,
            (cmv[7] >> 1) as i32,
            dst_buf,
            off + s16 + 16,
            stride,
            (cmv[6] & 1) != 0,
            (cmv[7] & 1) != 0,
        );
    } else {
        mc_luma_16x16(
            ref_buf,
            stride,
            (cmv[6] >> 1) as i32,
            (cmv[7] >> 1) as i32,
            scratch,
            0,
            16,
            (cmv[6] & 1) != 0,
            (cmv[7] & 1) != 0,
        );
    }
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
#[inline]
fn mc_only_chroma(
    ref_cr: &[u8],
    dst_cr: &mut [u8],
    ref_cb: &[u8],
    dst_cb: &mut [u8],
    off: usize,
    stride: usize,
    cmv: &[u32; 8],
    f: u32,
    scratch_cr: &mut [u8; 128],
    scratch_cb: &mut [u8; 128],
) {
    if (f & 4) == 0 {
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cr,
            stride,
            (cmv[0] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[1] >> 2) as i32,
            dst_cr,
            off,
            stride,
            cmv[0] & 3,
            cmv[1] & 3,
        );
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cr,
            stride,
            (cmv[2] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[3] >> 2) as i32,
            dst_cr,
            off + 8,
            stride,
            cmv[2] & 3,
            cmv[3] & 3,
        );
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cb,
            stride,
            (cmv[0] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[1] >> 2) as i32,
            dst_cb,
            off,
            stride,
            cmv[0] & 3,
            cmv[1] & 3,
        );
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cb,
            stride,
            (cmv[2] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[3] >> 2) as i32,
            dst_cb,
            off + 8,
            stride,
            cmv[2] & 3,
            cmv[3] & 3,
        );
    } else {
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cr,
            stride,
            (cmv[2] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[3] >> 2) as i32,
            scratch_cr,
            8,
            16,
            cmv[2] & 3,
            cmv[3] & 3,
        );
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cb,
            stride,
            (cmv[2] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[3] >> 2) as i32,
            scratch_cb,
            8,
            16,
            cmv[2] & 3,
            cmv[3] & 3,
        );
    }
    if (f & 3) == 0 {
        let s8 = stride * 8;
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cr,
            stride,
            (cmv[4] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[5] >> 2) as i32,
            dst_cr,
            off + s8,
            stride,
            cmv[4] & 3,
            cmv[5] & 3,
        );
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cr,
            stride,
            (cmv[6] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[7] >> 2) as i32,
            dst_cr,
            off + s8 + 8,
            stride,
            cmv[6] & 3,
            cmv[7] & 3,
        );
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cb,
            stride,
            (cmv[4] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[5] >> 2) as i32,
            dst_cb,
            off + s8,
            stride,
            cmv[4] & 3,
            cmv[5] & 3,
        );
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cb,
            stride,
            (cmv[6] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[7] >> 2) as i32,
            dst_cb,
            off + s8 + 8,
            stride,
            cmv[6] & 3,
            cmv[7] & 3,
        );
    } else {
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cr,
            stride,
            (cmv[6] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[7] >> 2) as i32,
            scratch_cr,
            8,
            16,
            cmv[6] & 3,
            cmv[7] & 3,
        );
        mc_chroma_8x8(
            ref_cb,
            stride,
            (cmv[6] >> 2) as i32,
            (cmv[7] >> 2) as i32,
            scratch_cb,
            8,
            16,
            cmv[6] & 3,
            cmv[7] & 3,
        );
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn slices_match_bink2_rules() {
        let kb2n = u32::from_le_bytes(*b"KB2n");
        let s1080 = compute_slices(kb2n, 0, 1920, 1080);
        assert_eq!(s1080.num_slices, 2);
        assert_eq!(&s1080.slice_ends[..2], &[544, 1088]);

        let s4k = compute_slices(kb2n, 0, 3840, 2160);
        assert_eq!(s4k.num_slices, 2);
        assert_eq!(&s4k.slice_ends[..2], &[1088, 2176]);

        let s4k_4 = compute_slices(kb2n, 2, 3840, 2160);
        assert_eq!(s4k_4.num_slices, 4);
        assert_eq!(&s4k_4.slice_ends[..4], &[544, 1088, 1632, 2176]);

        let small = compute_slices(kb2n, 0, 64, 64);
        assert_eq!(small.num_slices, 1);
        assert_eq!(small.slice_ends[0], 64);
    }

    #[test]
    fn bit_reader_cross_word_and_align() {
        let data = [0xAA, 0x55, 0xF0, 0x0F, 0x12, 0x34, 0x56, 0x78, 0x9A, 0xBC, 0xDE, 0xF1];
        let mut bs = BitReader::new(&data, 0);
        assert_eq!(bs.read_bits(4), 0xA);
        assert_eq!(bs.peek_bits(8), 0x5A);
        assert_eq!(bs.read_bits(12), 0x55A);
        assert_eq!(bs.read_bits(20), 0x20FF0);
        bs.align_u32();
        assert_eq!(bs.byte_pos(), 8);

        // Verify refill() followed by align_u32() preserves unread full word and exact byte_pos.
        let mut bs2 = BitReader::new(&data, 0);
        assert_eq!(bs2.read_bits(5), 0x0A);
        bs2.refill(); // loads second 32-bit word while first is partially consumed
        bs2.align_u32(); // discards remaining 27 bits of word 0, preserves word 1
        assert_eq!(bs2.byte_pos(), 4);
        assert_eq!(bs2.read_bits(8), 0x12);
        assert_eq!(bs2.read_bits(24), 0x785634);
        assert_eq!(bs2.byte_pos(), 8);
        assert_eq!(bs2.read_bits(32), 0xF1DEBC9A);
        assert_eq!(bs2.byte_pos(), 12);
    }
}

//! Clean-room bit-exact DSP primitives for the Bink 2 (`KB2f`..`KB2n`, integer path) video decoder.
//!
//! All stride parameters are expressed in **elements** (`u8` for byte planes, `i16` for residual
//! buffers). Arithmetic matches the reference x86-64 SSE2/SSSE3 routines bit-for-bit, including
//! `i16` wrapping operations, rounding biases, and fixed-point shifts.

use super::tables::{DEBLOCK_RESIDUAL_MASK, DEBLOCK_WEIGHTS};

/// 1D 8-point integer IDCT butterfly using `i16` wrapping arithmetic and arithmetic right shifts.
#[cfg_attr(target_arch = "aarch64", allow(dead_code))]
#[inline]
fn idct1d(x: [i16; 8]) -> [i16; 8] {
    let a0 = x[0].wrapping_add(x[4]);
    let a1 = x[0].wrapping_sub(x[4]);
    let a2 = (x[6] >> 1).wrapping_add(x[2]).wrapping_add(x[2] >> 2);
    let a3 = (x[2] >> 1).wrapping_sub(x[6].wrapping_add(x[6] >> 2));
    let b0 = a0.wrapping_add(a2);
    let b3 = a0.wrapping_sub(a2);
    let b1 = a1.wrapping_add(a3);
    let b2 = a1.wrapping_sub(a3);

    let p0 = x[3].wrapping_add(x[5]);
    let p1 = x[3].wrapping_sub(x[5]);
    let t1 = x[1].wrapping_add(p0);
    let t3 = x[1].wrapping_sub(p0);
    let t7 = p1.wrapping_add(x[7]);
    let t5 = p1.wrapping_sub(x[7]);

    let o0 = (t7 >> 2).wrapping_add(t1.wrapping_add(t1 >> 2).wrapping_sub(t1 >> 4));
    let o3 = (t1 >> 2).wrapping_sub(t7.wrapping_add(t7 >> 2).wrapping_sub(t7 >> 4));
    let o1 = t5.wrapping_sub(t5 >> 2).wrapping_sub(t5 >> 4).wrapping_add(t3);
    let o2 = t3.wrapping_sub(t3 >> 2).wrapping_sub(t3 >> 4).wrapping_sub(t5);

    [
        b0.wrapping_add(o0),
        b1.wrapping_add(o1),
        b2.wrapping_add(o2),
        b3.wrapping_add(o3),
        b3.wrapping_sub(o3),
        b2.wrapping_sub(o2),
        b1.wrapping_sub(o1),
        b0.wrapping_sub(o0),
    ]
}

/// 8x8 integer IDCT (scalar reference).
#[cfg_attr(target_arch = "aarch64", allow(dead_code))]
pub(crate) fn idct8x8_scalar(
    coeffs: &[i16; 64],
    out: &mut [i16],
    out_off: usize,
    out_stride: usize,
) {
    let mut p1 = [[0i16; 8]; 8];
    for v in 0..8 {
        let col_in = [
            coeffs[v],
            coeffs[8 + v],
            coeffs[16 + v],
            coeffs[24 + v],
            coeffs[32 + v],
            coeffs[40 + v],
            coeffs[48 + v],
            coeffs[56 + v],
        ];
        p1[v] = idct1d(col_in);
    }

    for x in 0..8 {
        let row_in = [
            p1[0][x], p1[1][x], p1[2][x], p1[3][x], p1[4][x], p1[5][x], p1[6][x], p1[7][x],
        ];
        let p2 = idct1d(row_in);
        for y in 0..8 {
            out[out_off + y * out_stride + x] = p2[y] >> 6;
        }
    }
}

/// 8x8 integer IDCT.
///
/// `coeffs` is indexed as `[u * 8 + v]` where `u` is X-frequency and `v` is Y-frequency.
/// Writes spatial `[y][x]` outputs (shifted by `>> 6`, no rounding bias) to `out`.
pub fn idct8x8(coeffs: &[i16; 64], out: &mut [i16], out_off: usize, out_stride: usize) {
    #[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
    {
        super::dsp_neon::idct8x8_neon(coeffs, out, out_off, out_stride);
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "aarch64"))]
    {
        idct8x8_scalar(coeffs, out, out_off, out_stride);
    }
}

/// Fast-path DC-only 8x8 IDCT (scalar reference).
#[cfg_attr(target_arch = "aarch64", allow(dead_code))]
pub(crate) fn idct8x8_dc_scalar(dc: i16, out: &mut [i16], out_off: usize, out_stride: usize) {
    let val = dc >> 6;
    for y in 0..8 {
        let row_start = out_off + y * out_stride;
        out[row_start..row_start + 8].fill(val);
    }
}

/// Fast-path DC-only 8x8 IDCT: fills an 8x8 tile with `dc >> 6`.
pub fn idct8x8_dc(dc: i16, out: &mut [i16], out_off: usize, out_stride: usize) {
    #[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
    {
        super::dsp_neon::idct8x8_dc_neon(dc, out, out_off, out_stride);
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "aarch64"))]
    {
        idct8x8_dc_scalar(dc, out, out_off, out_stride);
    }
}

/// Clamps a 16x8 `i16` block to `0..=255` and stores it into `dst` (scalar reference).
#[cfg_attr(target_arch = "aarch64", allow(dead_code))]
pub(crate) fn clamp_store_16x8_scalar(
    dst: &mut [u8],
    dst_off: usize,
    dst_stride: usize,
    src: &[i16],
    src_off: usize,
    src_stride: usize,
) {
    for r in 0..8 {
        let d_off = dst_off + r * dst_stride;
        let s_off = src_off + r * src_stride;
        let d_row = &mut dst[d_off..d_off + 16];
        let s_row = &src[s_off..s_off + 16];
        for c in 0..16 {
            d_row[c] = s_row[c].clamp(0, 255) as u8;
        }
    }
}

/// Clamps a 16x8 `i16` block to `0..=255` and stores it into `dst`.
pub fn clamp_store_16x8(
    dst: &mut [u8],
    dst_off: usize,
    dst_stride: usize,
    src: &[i16],
    src_off: usize,
    src_stride: usize,
) {
    #[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
    {
        super::dsp_neon::clamp_store_16x8_neon(dst, dst_off, dst_stride, src, src_off, src_stride);
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "aarch64"))]
    {
        clamp_store_16x8_scalar(dst, dst_off, dst_stride, src, src_off, src_stride);
    }
}

/// Adds a 16x8 `i16` residual to an 8-bit prediction block (scalar reference).
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
#[cfg_attr(target_arch = "aarch64", allow(dead_code))]
pub(crate) fn add_clamp_16x8_scalar(
    dst: &mut [u8],
    dst_off: usize,
    dst_stride: usize,
    res: &[i16],
    res_off: usize,
    res_stride: usize,
    pred: &[u8],
    pred_off: usize,
    pred_stride: usize,
) {
    for r in 0..8 {
        let d_off = dst_off + r * dst_stride;
        let r_off = res_off + r * res_stride;
        let p_off = pred_off + r * pred_stride;
        let d_row = &mut dst[d_off..d_off + 16];
        let r_row = &res[r_off..r_off + 16];
        let p_row = &pred[p_off..p_off + 16];
        for c in 0..16 {
            d_row[c] = (p_row[c] as i16).wrapping_add(r_row[c]).clamp(0, 255) as u8;
        }
    }
}

/// Adds a 16x8 `i16` residual to an 8-bit prediction block (wrapping in `i16`), clamps to
/// `0..=255`, and stores into `dst`.
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn add_clamp_16x8(
    dst: &mut [u8],
    dst_off: usize,
    dst_stride: usize,
    res: &[i16],
    res_off: usize,
    res_stride: usize,
    pred: &[u8],
    pred_off: usize,
    pred_stride: usize,
) {
    #[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
    {
        super::dsp_neon::add_clamp_16x8_neon(
            dst,
            dst_off,
            dst_stride,
            res,
            res_off,
            res_stride,
            pred,
            pred_off,
            pred_stride,
        );
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "aarch64"))]
    {
        add_clamp_16x8_scalar(
            dst,
            dst_off,
            dst_stride,
            res,
            res_off,
            res_stride,
            pred,
            pred_off,
            pred_stride,
        );
    }
}

/// Copies a 16x16 byte block from `src` to `dst` (scalar reference).
pub(crate) fn copy_16x16_scalar(
    src: &[u8],
    src_off: usize,
    src_stride: usize,
    dst: &mut [u8],
    dst_off: usize,
    dst_stride: usize,
) {
    for r in 0..16 {
        let s = src_off + r * src_stride;
        let d = dst_off + r * dst_stride;
        dst[d..d + 16].copy_from_slice(&src[s..s + 16]);
    }
}

/// Copies a 16x16 byte block from `src` to `dst`.
pub fn copy_16x16(
    src: &[u8],
    src_off: usize,
    src_stride: usize,
    dst: &mut [u8],
    dst_off: usize,
    dst_stride: usize,
) {
    #[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
    {
        super::dsp_neon::copy_16x16_neon(src, src_off, src_stride, dst, dst_off, dst_stride);
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "aarch64"))]
    {
        copy_16x16_scalar(src, src_off, src_stride, dst, dst_off, dst_stride);
    }
}

/// Copies a 16x8 byte block from `src` to `dst` (scalar reference).

        let s = src_off + r * src_stride;
        let d = dst_off + r * dst_stride;
        dst[d..d + 16].copy_from_slice(&src[s..s + 16]);
    }
}

pub fn copy_16x8(
